21:30:32 [INFO] FUNCTION: "pub fn eval_tokens(tokens: &[Token], mut i: usize) -> Result<(i32, usize), &'static str> {\n    // Doesnt really matter as long as result is idempotent for current_op\n    let mut result = 0;\n    let mut current_op = '+';\n\n    while i < tokens.len() {\n        match &tokens[i] {\n            Token::Number(n) => {\n                result = apply_operator(result, *n, current_op)?;\n                i += 1;\n            }\n            Token::Operator(op) => {\n                current_op = *op;\n                i += 1;\n            }\n            Token::Lparen => {\n                let (inner_result, next_i) = eval_tokens(tokens, i + 1)?;\n                result = apply_operator(result, inner_result, current_op)?;\n                i = next_i;\n            }\n            Token::Rparen => {\n                return Ok((result, i + 1));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok((result, i))\n}"
